-- FUNC-0901 ADOPTNODE
declare
  doc DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMDOCUMENT; other_doc DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMDOCUMENT;
  out_doc DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMDOCUMENT; doctype DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMDOCUMENTTYPE;
  out_doctype DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMDOCUMENTTYPE; root DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMELEMENT;
  new_elem DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMELEMENT; out_elem DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMELEMENT;
  node DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMNODE; child DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMNODE;
  new_node DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMNODE; other_node DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMNODE;
  out_node DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMNODE; attr DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMATTR;
  new_attr DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMATTR; out_attr DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMATTR;
  nl DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMNODELIST; out_nl DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMNODELIST;
  nnm DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMNAMEDNODEMAP; out_nnm DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMNAMEDNODEMAP;
  cd DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMCHARACTERDATA; out_cd DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMCHARACTERDATA;
  cdata DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMCDATASECTION; out_cdata DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMCDATASECTION;
  comment_node DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMCOMMENT; out_comment DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMCOMMENT;
  frag DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMDOCUMENTFRAGMENT; out_frag DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMDOCUMENTFRAGMENT;
  entity_node DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMENTITY; out_entity DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMENTITY;
  entity_ref DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMENTITYREFERENCE; out_entity_ref DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMENTITYREFERENCE;
  notation_node DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMNOTATION; out_notation DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMNOTATION;
  pi DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMPROCESSINGINSTRUCTION;
  out_pi DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMPROCESSINGINSTRUCTION;
  text_node DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMTEXT; out_text DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMTEXT;
  di DBMS_XMLDOM.DOMIMPLEMENTATION;
  out_text_value varchar2(32767); buffer_value varchar2(32767);
  out_int integer; out_bool boolean; cl clob; begin out_node:=DBMS_XMLDOM.ADOPTNODE(doc,other_node); end;
/
